### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%，顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域。數據公布後，市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫。聯準會理事鮑曼表示，決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果；舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示，不宜對升息過於保守，並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持 2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示，在能源價格波動下，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決，歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶；週二台股重挫近千點，外資大幅賣超台股 1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾 2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡，總成交量 91.2 億，再創歷史新高；週五外資持續匯出，台幣貶至周內低點 32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對 AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/19/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。上週人民幣中間價連續走升，週三中間價升至 6.7249 創
三年八個月新高，惟市場靜待美國公布通膨數據，交投謹慎，人民幣僅小幅走升；
週三晚間，美國公布 9 月 CPI 年增率 3.7%、核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，雙雙高於預期，
帶動美元指數升至年內高點 102.945、人民幣貶至周內低點 6.7005；然多位聯準會官
員發言表示聯準會不急於升息，令市場對於聯準會十月升息預期略有降溫，美元指
數自高點回落，激勵人民幣由貶轉升；臨近周末，聯準會主席華許於 IMF 會議發言
並未指引十月聯準會政策利率走向，再度淡化市場對於聯準會十月升息預期，國際
美元走勢偏弱，人民幣走升觸及年內高點 6.6825，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6850，週
線升值 91 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6870，週線升值 60 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。就國際市場來看，美國仍面臨高通膨、年底可能將
再次升息，利差因素支撐美元指數於高檔震盪，限縮人民幣升值空間；就中國國內
市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人
民幣表現；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，匯價維持在五日均線之下交
投，均線呈現空頭排列，加以 USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.6900 整數關卡，顯示 USD/CNH
空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6500-6.7000

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 先上後下。週初，受油價攀升推升美國通膨與利率預期帶動，美
元指數溫和走升創 6 月底以來新高，推升 USD/JPY 突破 158.50；週間，美國公布 9
月 CPI 高於預期，帶動美元指數升至 2025 年 5 月以來高點，也使 USD/JPY 最高來
到 158.94，然逢高獲利了結賣壓出籠使匯價回落；臨近週末，USD/JPY 一度下探至
157.53，隨後逢低買盤進場收復部分跌幅，終場收在 157.99，週線下跌 13 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、受美伊戰事推升汽柴油飆漲
與服務成本擴散影響，美國 9 月 CPI 及 PPI 月增漲幅均超出市場預期，加上初請失
業金人數連五週低於 20 萬人顯示勞動市場穩定，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月再次升
息，美日實質利差維持高檔，持續為 USD/JPY 提供下檔支撐。二、被視為鴿派的日
本經濟財政大臣城內實明確表態，貨幣政策應交由日本央行自行判斷，並宣告日本
已擺脫長期通縮、轉型為增長導向經濟體。此番言論降低政治干預日銀進一步升息
的疑慮，在貨幣政策正常化的腳步下，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間。三、就技
術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐
前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：157.00-159.00 
前週交易區間 
157.53 158.94

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
歐元兌美元匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初，持續受法國財政赤字與歐債利差擔憂拖累，
EUR/USD 震盪走弱；週間，因美國 9 月 CPI 高於預期，市場重新評估聯準會年底前
升息腳步，導致 EUR/USD 跳水最低來到 1.1112；臨近週末，儘管美國 PPI 與零售
銷售數據偏熱，市場優先反映美債殖利率回落與風險資產重新定價，美元多頭獲利
了結帶動 EUR/USD 谷底反彈，最高來到 1.1269，終場收在 1.1239，週線上漲 42 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、法國央行月度展望報
告指出，法國第三季 GDP 預估僅微幅成長 0.1%，印證近期高能源的成本已實質拖
累歐元區核心成員國增長。同時，歐洲央行總裁拉加德明確拒絕出手干預法債，要
求法國透過自身財政政策化解赤字困境；在明年法國大選前的財政隱憂與基本面疲
弱下，預計將令歐元上檔持續承壓。二、儘管歐洲央行管委溫施與總裁拉加德保留
12 月升息預期，加上德國基建與軍費支出為歐元提供部分下檔保護，但市場穩固預
期聯準會年底前將再升息一次。在美國經濟韌性與高利差優勢主導下，預計將限縮
歐元反彈空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 自低點反彈並站回五日線
之上，然而中長期均線下彎仍維持空頭排列，RSI 指標亦處於 50 中軸下方，顯示整
體空方趨勢尚未扭轉，反彈至月線附近恐面臨較大賣壓。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1300 
前週交易區間 
1.1112 1.1269

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115. 10.12-115.10.16 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.020~2.025% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本週央行連續減發定存單使市場資金寬鬆，雖外資大舉匯
出，資金供給仍充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週五央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.58%，低於市場
預期區間 1.60%-1.62%，較上期標售結果下跌 5.8 個基本點。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 5 年期指標券 A15108 週二搓合成交在 1.88%，後隨美債利率滑落，加上本週
2 年期公債標債結果提振市場持券信心，帶動 10 年期指標券 A15109 週四向
下成交在 2.02%~2.025%。 
在銀行去化短期資金及補充 LCR 之需求下，週三 2 年期公債得標利率為 1.65%，
落在市場預估區間下緣，投標倍數回升至 2.86 倍，銀行業得標比重上升至 88%。
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率大幅回檔，促使台債交投偏空氛圍轉趨和
緩，惟在央行維持緊縮貨幣政策下，買盤積極追價的意願不強，預期短線台債
殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
2357 華碩電腦(ASUS)

報告日期：2026/11/17 研究員：曾智楷(Kevin Tseng) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 品牌營收 2588 億，QoQ +7% ，毛利率 15.9.6%，EPS 
29.10 元。 
 公司預估 4Q26 伺服器營收將季增 100%，由於部分客戶第三
季因建置時程延後，使伺服器出貨與營收遞延至第四季認列。
PC 與零組件業務方面，受到記憶體等零組件價格上漲壓抑終
端市場需求，預估 4Q26 電腦業務營收季減 15%，零組件則預
估將季減 10%。 
 受惠於 GPU AI 伺服器需求暢旺，公司預估 2026 全年伺服器
營收將成長 150%，並 預 估 2027 年伺服器營收可望維持雙位數
至高雙位數的年增率。

一. 受惠伺服器、PC 業務成長，3Q26 EPS 29.1 元 
華碩 3Q26 品牌營收 2587.9 億元，QoQ +7%，YoY +36%，主要
受惠於伺服器、PC 業務成長，帶動第三季營收創歷史新高。就產
品組合來看，3Q26 系統產品(System)佔整體營收的 50%，開放
平台(Open Platform) 佔 20%，基礎設施解決方案事業群(IS)佔
30%。公司表示，面對 DRAM 等零組件漲價逆風，第三季 PC 營
收仍年增超過 25% ，主要由於公司高價值產品，包含電競
Gaming、高階 AI PC 及商用 PC，佔 PC 營收比重超過 65%，且
商用 PC 出貨量維持強勁，年成長超過 30%。另外，伺服器業務
方面，儘管部分訂單遞延至第四季認列，第三季伺服器營收仍實
現超過 100%的年增長；通用型 CPU 伺服器亦受惠於企業擴建地
端資料中心以支援 AI 工作負載，營收年增超過 80%。獲利方面，
3Q26 營業毛利 412 億，QoQ +3%，YoY +68%，毛利率 15.9%，
QoQ -0.6ppts，YoY +3.0ppts，營業利益 219.6 億，QoQ +12%，
YoY +161%，營利率 8.5%，QoQ +0.4ppts，YoY +4.1ppts，3Q26
業外利益 35.9 億，稅後淨利 216.3 億，QoQ +14%，YoY +105%，
單季稅後 EPS 29.1 元。

三. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 89.30/86.35 元 
展望 2026 年，在伺服器業務方面，公司表示目前整體訂單能見度
強勁，除了受惠於 GPU AI 伺服器需求暢旺之外，企業擴建地端資
料中心，亦帶動通用型 CPU 伺服器需求同步提升。在第四季翻倍
季增帶動下，公司預估 2026 全年伺服器營收達成至少 150%的年
成長目標。而 PC 與零組件業務，則受記憶體等零組件價格大幅
上漲影響，在 4Q26 至 1H27 的 PC 市場需求逆風與價格成本混亂
期間，PC 業務的獲利能力與營業利益率將承受一定壓力。預估公
司 2026 全年營收 1.05 兆，YoY +42.5%，毛利率 15.8%，營利率
7.2%，稅後淨利 663 億，稅後 EPS 89.30 元。展望 2027 年，PC
整體市場受零組件價格上漲與需求定價混亂影響，公司預估 2027
年全球 PC 市場整體出貨量將年減 10%至 15%，就季度節奏來看，
上半年跌幅較大，下半年將逐步回升。而伺服器業務方面，管理
層則預估 2027 年伺服器營收可望維持雙位數至高雙位數的年增
率。由於公司客戶涵蓋 Tier 2/3 CSP、NeoCloud、大型科技公司、
AI 研究機構、企業地端資料中心及國家級主權 AI 專案，儘管近期
市場面臨資本市場融資條件趨緊以及美國期中大選後各地資料中
心建置監管趨嚴等逆風，但目前訂單需求並未出現明顯延遲或變
化。預估公司 2027 全年營收 1.26 兆，YoY +19.5% ，毛利率
14.5%，營利率 6.6%，稅後淨利 641 億，稅後 EPS 86.35 元。

四. 投資建議為 750~950 元區間操作 
公司 AI 伺服器業務維持強勁成長動能，預期 2027 年將維持雙位
數成長表現，但考量到記憶體缺貨情況延續，可能持續影響公司
PC 業務的營運動能。公司目前股價交易於 2027 EPS 約 9~10 倍
本益比，評價上並未明顯偏低，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價
750-950 元(9-11x 2027 EPS)。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華碩電腦(ASUS)為全球知名筆電品牌，以自有品牌「ASUS」銷
售全球。主要營業項目為 3C 資訊產品(含電腦系統產品、主機板
及各類板卡及智慧型手機等手持裝置等)之設計、研發及銷售。

二.產業趨勢概況 
1.3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量暴跌逾二成，AI 搶走記憶體成衰退元兇 
受 AI 資料中心擴張排擠記憶體與零組件供給影響，2026 年第三
季全球 PC 市場旺季不旺，根據 IDC，3Q26 PC 單季出貨量降至
6,270 萬台，年減 20.1%。由於 DRAM 與 SSD 價格暴漲逾 4 倍，
在 PC 成本占比由 15%飆升至近 40%，加上處理器與顯示卡等元
件短缺，以及業者於上半年提前拉貨透支需求，導致整體市場面
臨沉重的庫存與成本壓力。就各大品牌來看，第三季普遍陷入雙
位數的衰退，聯想第三季出貨量年減-22.6%、惠普-30.9%、戴爾
-25%、蘋果-11.3%，而華碩則展現出強勁的韌性，第三季出貨量
達 550 萬台，年減 8.6%，為全球前五大 PC 品牌中唯一跌幅控制
在個位數的業者，表現明顯優於其他競爭對手。然而研究機構仍

2. 明年 CSP Capex 預估破 1.5 兆美元，AI 伺服器產值年增逾 9 成 
根據集邦科技(TrendForce)最新研究，受惠於 Google、Amazon、
Meta、Microsoft、Oracle 及中國四大科技巨頭等全球前九大雲端
服務供應商(CSP)擴大投資 AI 基礎設施，預估 2026 年九大 CSP
資本支出總額將逾 9,570 億美元，YoY +106%，2027 年更因伺服
器整機漲價、零組件缺貨漲價及供應商要求預付款等因素，有望
突破 1.5 兆美元，YoY +60%，雙雙優於預期。在超大型 CSP、Tier-2
資料中心與主權 AI 等需求帶動下，預估 2026 年 AI 伺服器產值約
4,790 億美元，占整體伺服器產值近 75%；2027 年隨輝達 GB300、
VR200 與超微 Helios 等整櫃式方案成為主流，加上記憶體等關鍵
零組件短缺推升單價，預計整體伺服器產值將首度跨越 1 兆美元，
其中 AI 伺服器產值將躍升至近 9,200 億美元 YoY +90%，占比進
一步提高至 81%左右。

圖二、2024-2027 年九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估(單位:十億美元)

3. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型(LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28%更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展，2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

Document 149: 261117_daiwa_merida

### Page 1

Summary: Merida hosted its 3Q26 investor call on 17 November after market hours, 
following release of its 3Q26 results on 11 November (see our memo). Merida’s gross 
margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our estimate 
(16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%). Factoring in the equity investment loss of 
TWD150m+, its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the 
consensus estimate (TWD1.65).

We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on this company, please 
refer to our latest flash note, 2Q26 review: mainly dragged by Specialized, on 14 August.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results recap. Merida’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD6,098m (-12.1% QoQ, -15.6% 
YoY) missed our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 13.6% and 11.1%, 
respectively, which we attributed to the discount when destocking the older models. Its 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%), due to less OBM sales, the price discount 
for further inventory digestion and the depreciation of the Euro. Factoring in the equity 
investment loss of TWD150m+, its net profit of TWD176m (-44.8% QoQ, -71.9% YoY) 
missed our and the consensus estimates by 65.0% and 65.6%. Its 3Q26 EPS was 
TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65).  
• 2026 outlook update. Merida revised down its 2026 full-year shipments to 960k units 
(Taiwan plant 280k, down from previous 340k; China plant 680k, down from previous 
700k), as it continues to manage its existing model shipments and expects new models 
in 2027 to bring stronger demand. The company now sees recovery in Europe and 
China, with inventory digestion coming to an end: 9M26 sales in Europe grew 14% YoY, 
driven by strong demand for road bikes and gravel bikes; 9M 26 sales in China grew by 
1% while shipments grew by 7% (vs. revenue guidance of declining 15-20% at the 
beginning of 2026), driven by low- and mid-end models. 
• SBC expected to be profitable in 4Q26. Due to SBC’s conservative tax assumption 
(over 100% effective tax rate) in 1Q-3Q26, if and when the tax rate realised at year-end 
is determined to be less than currently allocated for, we expect there will be a huge profit 
recognised in 4Q26 to offset the estimated after-tax loss recognised in 1Q-3Q26. Merida 
and SBC will review the tax assumption policy after full-year results are out, as the 
policy has caused a great deal of investor confusion. Including USD15m of tariff refund 
in 3Q26, SBC’s pre-tax profit grew by 42% YoY for 9M26. Merida expects there is 
another USD5m of tariff refund to collect in 4Q26.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 
13.2x/10.6x, based on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER 
trading range of 7-33x. Key downside risks: slower-than-expected e-bike penetration in the 
EU and the US, worse-than-expected demand in an inflationary environment and inventory 
digestion.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 1

Update
King Yuan Electronics Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Continuing capacity build with unchanged 2027 demand: The company mentioned
that AI demand remains strong and customers (especially the largest customer) are
still asking for more clean room build. 2027 demand is almost confirmed with no
changes over the past month.
Capacity allocation: US capacity will fully support the largest customer initially and
the capacity in Taiwan is fungible between different customers. For example, given

4Q26 weakness for consumer/smartphones, the company has shifted spare capacity
to meet other demand, including the testers from Advantest and Teradyne.
Margin should still be in the upward trend: The priority for the company now is to
have customers consign the equipment if they are asking for more capacity, lowering
the deprecation pressure. Also, given the tight capacity, the company would serve
the products with higher margin (generally AI demand and longer test time product)

the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.

### Page 1

ab 23 November 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Elite Material
Manifesting AI-driven upcycle; initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL - EMC: major beneficiary of  structural CCL material upgrade
We initiate EMC with a Buy rating and PT NT$5,000. The company is a global CCL leader
that enables AI systems with high-speed and low-loss materials. We see booming GPU/
ASIC demand driving not just CCL consumption but also a shift toward higher-grade

CCL products to meet high-speed and signal integrity requirements. M8 CCL has
become standard for Blackwell and will migrate to M9/M10 for Feynman.  We anticipate
these structural upgrades and capacity expansion to support  62% sales CAGR   through
'26-'28E; and anticipate product mix shift toward  M7+ to drive margin expansion.
Resilient fundamentals underpinned by strong positioning
Our industry studies suggest EMC is well positioned across AI GPU/ASIC projects. It was

the dominant supplier for Nvidia Hopper OAM/UBB boards. Doosan has begun to supply
compute tray from Blackwell, while EMC and SYTECH supplies the switch tray. We
believe EMC is also one of the major suppliers for Google TPU (splitting with Panasonic)
and AWS Trainium (other suppliers including a TW CCL supplier with potential new
entrants such as SYTECH and Nan Ya Plastics). We see AI platforms ramping up
production in H226 that are enlarging the supply/demand imbalance, driving further

CCL pricing uplifts. We believe EMC's capacity scale and major projects' share allocation
backed by its upstream sourcing will support its growth realization of strong AI demand.
Investing in M8+ for future growth with margin expansion
As a major supplier for  GPU and ASIC platforms, EMC is expanding capacity to capture
future growth. The company announced an investment total of NT$98.6bn through
2027-2030F to add capacity across Taiwan, Mainland China, and Malaysia, where its

total capacity will increase from 5,850k sheets/month in 2026F to 15,250k sheets/
month by 2030F, and these additions would be supporting M8+ CCL demand. M7+
increased from 55–60% of product mix in Q126 to 65–70% in Q326. Looking ahead,
management guides for M7+ to reach 70–80%  in 2027F as AI server specs continue to
move up the material ladder. We expect earnings to grow at 82% CAGR through 2026-
2028E, outpacing topline growth of 62% for the same periods, supported by favourable

product mix shift. Meanwhile, EMC is attempting to build ABF substrate CCL capacity
and going through qualification in H226. We believe high-end CCL remains its key focus
and note that these capacity additions are fungible for M8/M9 high-speed CCL
production, and we have not yet factored in ABF substrate CCL business potential.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with PT$5,000
In light of market concerns on AI slowdown, we note that our estimates are below

consensus factoring in slower capacity ramp (10-30% lower than announced plans).
Our NT$5,000 PT is based on 12x 2028E PE, which we think is conservative at the low-
end of its  historical trading range of 7-20x. Despite more conservative estimates, still see
attractive risk-reward back by  37%'26-'30E earnings CAGR, implying only 0.3x PEG.

### Page 2

Elite Material UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is favourable pricing environment and current CCL industry upcycle sustainable?
We continue to see AI CCL content value increases as computing and networking/interconnect
demands surges. Extended equipment lead time and qualification process along with increasing
manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more constrained upstream inputs. We expect

persisting CCL industry shortage to drive better pricing power and believe high-end CCL have
entered a spec-driven structural upgrade cycle, not a temporary commodity uptick.
Q: Could high-end CCL drive the next phase of growth for Elite Material?
Yes. Among Taiwanese suppliers, we believe EMC is best positioned to capitalise on rapid AI
deployment based on its competitive high-end material offerings across GPU/ASIC platforms. We
expect EMC will continue to benefit from growing AI-driven computing/networking demand and

high-end CCL spec upgrades as a key supplier for major GPU and several US cloud service providers'
ASIC CCL materials. We are positive on its solid business fundamentals and see its business scale,
customer exposure, further pricing uplifts, favourable product mix shift could drive strong earnings
expansion in the next few years.
Among CCL supply chain, we believe EMC is best positioned for further re-rating given its leading
position in high-end CCL materials with capacity scale, high-end product offerings, and sourcing

capability. The company has board customer exposure among major GPU and ASIC platforms, best
positioned to capitalize AI-driven spec upgrades.
Top 6 CSP capex spending has revised up another leg, strengthening visibility into AI-related CCL
demand. Hyperscaler capex grew 25% QoQ/ 101% YoY in Q326 to US$252.7bn, with UBS 2026/27E
forecasts revised up 2%/18% to imply +96%/+74% YoY growth to US$897bn/US$1.6tn.
Meanwhile, the pricing backdrop remains supportive,   with Taiwanese CCL suppliers raising prices by

10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit increases in August and
September. EMC’s latest commentary reinforces this trend,  noting 5-20% price increases across its
portfolio in Q426F and further upside possible should the supply-demand mismatch persist.
EMC’s share price has fallen 56% from its Aug peak (vs. TAIEX -21%), amid the broader tech sell-off
and rising concerns over slower AI spending. The stock now trades at 10x/7x our 2027/2028E P/E,

below its long-term historical avg. of 13.5x and the lower-end of the  AI cycle 10-48x, despite our
'27/'28 EPS estimates already  c.4%/4% below consensus. We believe current valuation increasingly
prices in slower AI demand, while giving insufficient credit to EMC’s technology leadership and
continued M8/M9/M10 upgrades, which we believe warrant a premium to CCL peers. Reflecting
recent market sentiment, we conservatively value EMC at  12x 2028E P/E, around its historical

median. This remains attractive given 37% 2026-30E earnings CAGR and implies  0.3x PEG.

Elite Material  was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 and started as a conventional FR-4 copper clad thin
core laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC has established its global presence as the

world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs with its leading technology expertise. In
2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellite accounted for 65-
70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and automation/industrial/others (5-10%).

Company Description

### Page 3

Elite Material UBS Research
business overview
Founded in 1992, EMC  started as a manufacturer of conventional FR-4 CCL and
prepregs used in printed circuit boards. The company began moving up the value chain
with its entry into HDI in 1997, followed by  the introduction of halogen-free materials
licensed from Hitachi Chemical in 2002, allowing EMC to establish a leading position in
handset HDI applications. Smartphones subsequently became EMC's core franchise with

the company sharing c70% market share and becoming a key supplier to Apple as well
as leading Android brands.
EMC began its next major product transition in 2017, when it entered the high-speed
digital materials market with ~0% market share. Over the following seven years, the
company expanded its low-loss material portfolio and customer qualifications across
high-speed switches, general servers and cloud infrastructure, before benefiting from
the rapid growth of AI servers and CSP ASIC platforms. EMC’s high-speed digital market

share reached 28% in 2023, when it became the global No.1 supplier, and increased
further to 38.6% in 2025. This transition has materially reshaped its revenue mix:
infrastructure increased from around 30% of revenue in 2019-21 to 75-80% currently,
while handset exposure declined from 50% to 10-15%. EMC’s customer base has
consequently broadened from handset-centric exposure toward leading networking
and cloud customers, including NVIDIA-related AI platforms and major CSP ASIC

programs. Building on its HDI expertise, EMC further expanded into IC substrate
materials in 2020, and has grown its market share to 1.6% by 2025 where it is now the
only non-Japanese/Korean player with substrate material technology expertise.

HDI leadership underpins EMC’s longstanding consumer-tech franchise
EMC has historically been the global leader in mobile-device applications, with 70%

market share in mobile-phone CCL and over 80% in 5G smartphone CCL. Mobile
devices remain a meaningful 10-15% of EMC's Q326 sales, supported by  its
longstanding leadership in HDI materials, where it has ranked No.1 globally for more
than 20 years with over 70% share. EMC began developing conventional HDI in 1997,
followed by Anylayer HDI in 2010 and mSAP in 2017, progressively enabling finer lines
and higher circuit density. Unlike high-layer-count (HLC) boards, which primarily add

layers to accommodate more circuitry, HDI uses microvias, finer routing and sequential
lamination to pack more interconnects into a smaller board area, making it particularly
well suited to smartphones, tablets and notebooks where miniaturization is critical. The
repeated lamination and finer structures also place greater demands on CCL, including
dimensional stability, resin-flow control, low CTE, CAF resistance and adhesion, creating
meaningful materials know-how rather than simply a different PCB process. This

expertise helped EMC establish an early position in the Apple supply chain, when
handheld devices rose to  50% of sales in 2016.  EMC’s  historical smartphone customer
base has included Apple, Samsung, Huawei, HTC and Nokia. While consumer tech is no
longer EMC's growth focus, we view it as a stable and strategically important revenue
base, complementing the companies rapidly expanding infrastructure focus.

### Page 4

Figure 3: EMC's technology advancements
Source: Company data, courtesy of EMC
Riding the AI-driven CCL upgrade cycle, with M9 extending high-end leadership
AI infrastructure has become EMC’s core growth engine, with infrastructure reaching
roughly 75-80% of revenue in Q326 and growing 183% YoY, driven primarily by
accelerating AI server and high-speed networking demand. EMC has translated this
demand into a leading position in high-end materials, capturing 38.6% of the global

high-speed halogen-free CCL market in 2025, which should enable the company to
continue outgrowing the broader market through both share gains and content
expansion. Industry forecasts cited by the company call for high-end CCL market to
grow at a 40% CAGR in 2024-2027F and AI CCL at a 178% CAGR in 2025-2027F, while
UBS expects hyperscaler capex to grow +96%/+74% YoY in 2026/27E. As AI spending
broadens across both  GPUs and ASICs, we expect EMC to benefit from rising system

volumes as well as increasing CCL content and value per system.
The latter is driven by a structural migration toward higher-grade, lower-loss materials as
data rates and PCB complexity increase. M7-and-above extreme/super low-loss products
already account for 65-70% of EMC's revenue in Q326, with management expecting
the mix to reach over 70% in 2027. Based on our industry checks, the economic value
creation is non-linear across the material stack. While M7 typically carries a 15-50% ASP

premium over M6, M8 pricing is roughly twice that of M7. The largest step-up occurs at
M9, where ASPs are approximately 5-6x those of M6 and GMs exceed 50% versus
roughly 25-30% for M6 and over 30% for M7. Consequently, EMC's earnings sensitivity
to high-end mix expansion is substantially greater than revenue growth alone would
imply.  M9 represents the next leg of this upgrade cycle. EMC was the first supplier to
complete customer qualification from NVIDIA and expects volume shipments to begin in

H226. Management expects M9 to contribute a mid- to high-single-digit percentage of
revenue in H226 before reaching double digits in 2027. EMC is still the first source for
M9 grade applications  including selected NVIDIA Rubin  boards and next-generation
hyperscaler ASIC platforms, the competitors include potential second source Shengyi
and ITEQ who have also passed qualifications.
Nvidia: sustaining a strong position across successive platform upgrades. EMC’s

position in AI CCL was established in 2023, when it became the primary supplier of high-
speed CCL for Nvidia’s H100 GPU server boards. We believe EMC’s prior track record in
high-end HDI helped position it well as GPU boards moved toward greater layer counts,
multiple lamination and more demanding signal-integrity requirements. Nvidia's
architecture has evolved since then and the supplier landscape has become more
differentiated by board. For GB200/GB300, Doosan is the primary compute-tray CCL

supplier, while EMC is the main supplier for the switch tray, alongside Shengyi. Moving
into Vera Rubin, EMC is positioned for CPX/midplane and switch-tray applications, while
Doosan remains dominant in the compute tray. We believe its over 50% share in
NVIDIA's server CCL should remain stable as it benefits from the annual cadence
upgrades.

### Page 5

ASIC: broad hyperscaler exposure reduces reliance on Nvidia. EMC holds roughly
70% share of ASIC-server CCL, supported by exposure across leading hyperscaler
programs. Beyond Nvidia, EMC is benefiting from the continued ramp of custom silicon
at AWS, Google and Meta, alongside a migration to higher-specification materials from
M7 to M8/M8+. Key growth drivers include AWS Trainium 3, Google TPU v8 shipments
from H226, and expanding deployments of Meta's in-house accelerators. With multiple

custom-silicon platforms scaling concurrently, we expect ASIC demand to remain a
meaningful contributor through 2027, further broadening EMC's participation in
hyperscaler AI spending.
Switch: 800G/1.6T migration drives multi-year upgrade cycle
Switch-related materials account for roughly 15-20% of EMC’s infrastructure revenue.
As the connectivity backbone of AI clusters and cloud data centers, switches are seeing
rapidly rising bandwidth requirements, driving the transition from 400G toward 800G

and ultimately 1.6T. 400G remains the largest contributor today, while 800G began
ramping in 2025 and is expected to take around three years to become mainstream, and
1.6T adoption should begin in 2H26 on a similar trajectory. We expect these overlapping
upgrade cycles to support both volume growth and richer material content over the next
several years.
LEO satellite adds a niche but scalable high-speed CCL opportunity
LEO satellite has developed into a meaningful niche within EMC’s infrastructure

portfolio, accounting for roughly 6% of 2025 revenue, providing  materials across
satellite equipment, ground receiving stations, and user terminal equipment. EMC
began targeting the segment in 2023 and entered mass production for LEO satellite
materials in 2024, alongside completion of next-generation product qualification. The
opportunity fits well with EMC’s existing strengths in high-speed, low-loss laminates
and HDI, as satellite boards require strong signal integrity, thermal reliability and

compact, high-density designs.  Demand is supported by continued constellation
deployment from two major US customers, while material specifications are also
upgrading from M6 to M7 as transmission speeds increase, providing additional CCL
content per satellite. Key competitors include US-based Isola which has an established
presence in aerospace and satellite-grade high-performance laminates. We  see LEO as a
relatively small but differentiated growth driver that broadens EMC’s infrastructure

exposure beyond AI servers and switches.
Substrates represent an emerging growth leg with significant share gain
potential
Substrates remain a relatively small part of Elite Material's business today, but the
company has steadily expanded its market share since entering the market in 2020 to
1.6% in 2025. IC substrate laminates carry significantly higher technical barriers than
conventional PCB laminates, with demanding qualification requirements and a

concentrated supplier base historically dominated by Japanese and Korean vendors
including Resonac, Mitsubishi Gas Chemical (MGC), and Doosan  etc. Ongoing substrate
material shortages have led several customers to qualify alternative suppliers, creating
an opportunity for EMC to penetrate programs supported by leveraging its longstanding
HDI and mSAP expertise as a natural pathway into substrate materials. We believe the
company is currently qualifying materials for AI ASIC-related substrate applications, with

initial shipments potentially commencing following customer approval. Looking
forward, EMC's first milestone is to reach 5% substrate market share, followed by 10%,
with both targets expected to be achieved within the next 12-24 months. We believe
successful execution against these milestones would establish substrates as a
meaningful incremental growth driver and further expand EMC's exposure to AI
infrastructure content growth.

### Page 6

EMC consists of three main business segments: Infrastructure, Mobile Devices, and
Automotive/Industrial.
1. Infrastructure (75-80% of Q326 revenue):
 AI Server (55-60% of Infrastructure)
 General Server (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 Switch (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 LEO satellite (5-10% of Infrastructure)
2. Mobile Devices (10-15% of Q326 revenue). Mobile device applications include
smartphones & tablets, notebooks and memory modules. These products utilize

HDI PCB designs to support device miniaturization, higher circuit density and high-
speed signal transmission.
3. Automotive/Industrial (5-10% of Q326 revenue). Automotive and industrial
applications include autonomous driving (ADAS), electric vehicles (power
conversion and charging), medical devices and industrial electronics, which require
high-reliability PCB materials for demanding operating environments.
Ongoing capacity expansions across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, and

a unique US production presence
EMC was founded in Taoyuan, Taiwan in 1992, where its headquarters and original CCL/
prepreg manufacturing base remain today. In 2005, EMC added its Hsinchu plant to
provide mass-lamination services for PCB customers requiring outsourced capacity. The
company has since built three major production sites in China, Kunshan in 1999,
Zhongshan in 2005, and Huangshi in 2019, serving Northern/Eastern, Southern and
Central China, respectively. In 2024, EMC established its Penang, Malaysia plant, its first

production base in Southeast Asia. In the US, EMC acquired Arlon EMD in 2020, making
it the only Asian CCL supplier with an active US production presence. In July 2026, EMC
raised planned investment in the site to US$69mn, with the expanded facility targeted
to commence operations from H226.
Management has laid out a substantial multi-year capacity expansion plan to capture
robust AI infrastructure demand and sustained tightness in high-end CCL. In Oct 2026,

the board approved an additional ~NT$98.6bn capex budget for phased factory
expansion across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, underscoring the scale of
EMC’s capacity build-out. Laminate capacity is expected to increase from 5.85mn
sheets/month in 2026E to 8.70mn in 2027E (+49% YoY), 10.45mn in 2028E (+20%
YoY), 11.95mn in 2029E (+14% YoY) and 15.25mn by 2030E (+28% YoY), implying a
~27% CAGR over 2026-30E.
EMC has nevertheless adjusted the phasing of certain expansion plans as it encountered

longer-than-expected expansion lead times in Taiwan. Most notably, the initial 300k
sheets/month Guanyin addition, previously targeted for Dec 2026, has been pushed
back by ~6 months due to labor/material constraints and longer government safety
inspections, while a further 600k sheets/month addition planned for 2028 has shifted to
2029. By contrast, capacity expansion at Kunshan, China can proceed more quickly.
Demand visibility remains robust, with signed LTAs exceeding 9.4mn sheets of

incremental capacity and supporting full utilization through 2028. Customers are
increasingly entering multi-year LTAs to secure high-end CCL supply, while management
continues to secure LTAs and verbal commitments beyond 2028 and sees customer
demand extending through 2030.

Shareholder structure:
Publicly listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in Nov 1998, EMC is chaired by Ting-Yu
Tung who directly  holds ~1.5% of outstanding shares. Related investment vehicles Yu
Chang Investment and Yu Sheng Investment together hold ~10.7%, bringing the
chairman/family-related holding to 12%. FINI ownership peaked at ~55% in 2017 amid
strong smartphone/HDI demand, before declining on smartphone weakness and Apple
SLP share loss. It bottomed at ~24% in 2024 amid concerns over Blackwell CCL share

loss to Korean competitors, but has since recovered to ~41% (vs Taiex's FINI holding
47%), supported by stronger-than-expected AI server/ASIC and high-end CCL demand.

Elite Material UBS Research
Financial analysis
Q326 earnings beat on strong GM expansion and high-end mix. EMC delivered
record-high Q326 revenue of NT$60.9bn, up 29% QoQ and 142% YoY, driven by
strong infrastructure demand, price increases and favorable product mix. Gross margin
expanded 3.8ppts QoQ to 37.7%, with pricing and mix each contributing roughly half
of the improvement, while operating margin reached 30.7% and EPS rose to NT$40.47.
Infrastructure remained the core growth engine at 75-80% of revenue, with sales up

32% QoQ/176% YoY, while  full utilization underscored continued supply-demand
tightness amid strong high-end CCL demand.
Q426 to see continued pricing and mix momentum. For Q426E, EMC guided for
15-20% QoQ revenue growth and 38.0-39.5% GM, supported by continued price
increases and favorable product mix. By application, management expects infrastructure
revenue to grow ~20% QoQ, mobile to remain flattish, and automotive/industrial/
others to decline 5-10% QoQ, with the latter reflecting lower volumes as capacity is

reallocated toward high-speed CCL. We forecast Q426 revenue of NT$69.4bn (+14%
QoQ), GM to increase 0.5ppts to 38.2% and EPS of NT$47.03 (+16% QoQ). EMC plans
5-20% price increases across its portfolio in Q4, with pricing reflecting raw-material cost
increases; management also noted further upside to pricing if the supply-demand
imbalance persists.

Revenue and GM and EPS forecasts for 2027/2028E
We forecast EMC’s revenue to grow 80%/45% YoY in 2027/2028E, supported by
substantial capacity expansion, continued pricing and sustained high-end CCL demand.
Capacity is expected to increase ~49%/20% YoY in 2027/2028, with new capacity
coming online progressively and management expecting utilization to remain full

through 2028. EMC is accelerating investment to capture this demand, with capex rising
from NT$28bn in 2026 to NT$53bn in both 2027 and 2028, although FCF is expected to
remain negative for two years before turning positive in 2028-2029. Management
indicated signed LTAs already exceed 9.4mn sheets of incremental capacity, providing
strong visibility into the ramp, while substrate laminate represents additional upside,
with a new ASIC project expected to complete qualification by end-2026 and begin

shipments in 2027.
Pricing and product mix should provide additional growth on top of the capacity ramp.
EMC expects high-end CCL tightness to persist for the next two years, as extended
equipment lead times constrain the pace of new supply, while E-glass tightness could

### Page 9

persist until H227. Management sees further room for price increases if the supply-
demand imbalance persists. Product mix should provide another ASP and margin
tailwind, with M7+ expected to rise from 65-70% of Q326 sales to 70-80% in 2027. M9
began shipments in H226 and is expected to enter MP in 2027, while M10 qualification
is targeted for completion by end-2026, enabling initial shipments in H127 ahead of MP
in H227. With  typical generational upgrades carrying at least a 30% price increase, we

forecast GM to expand from 35.7% in 2026E to 40.5%/42.0% in 2027/2028E and
OPM to rise from 28.6% to 34.2%/36.2%, reflecting continued pricing, richer high-end
mix and operating leverage, driving EPS of NT$280.82/430.74, up 116%/53% YoY,
respectively. Our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are c.4%/4% below consensus, reflecting
more conservative utilization and pricing assumptions to account for potential downside
from slower AI/NVIDIA demand, while we remain positive on EMC’s technology

leadership and high-end product positioning.

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data

centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and
Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting

conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK).  This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data
center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land supply limits. We

### Page 10

believe the market continues to expect a strong 2027 but is now more cautious for
2028. We think some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as
the UBS team has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable
capex for 2028. That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts that
might cap hyperscalers from securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water
permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending

materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan falls short of expectations...: The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 4.95mn sheets in 2025 to
10.45mn/15.25mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. Our current model assumes its CCL
monthly capacity of 10.08mn sheets in 2028E, and note that if capacity additions
were only 50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario,

this will imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 25%/35%/39%/43% to our
current estimates.
 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on

AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by
5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this will imply potential earnings
downside of 32%/45%/53%/60% to our current 2028E estimates.

EMC Share price EMC P/E (x) +1 Std Dev =20x
EMC Avg P/E  14x -1 Std Dev =7x
EMC P/E (x)EMC share price (NT$)
Source: Refinitiv Eikon
EMC’s 12-month forward P/E historically traded below 20x prior to 2023, before re-

rating with the onset of the AI cycle. The re-rating accelerated from 2025 through H126,
driven by 1) EMC’s leadership in M8 and expectations that this would extend into M9; 2)
growing exposure to hyperscaler ASIC programmes and expectations of regaining a
leading supplier position in NVIDIA’s Rubin platform; and 3) rising CCL content per
system as AI architectures adopt higher-specification materials and more complex board
designs. Expectations for CCL price increases and EMC’s technology and capacity

advantages provided further support in H126, taking its forward P/E to a historical peak
of 47x in April 2026. Since the AI cycle began, EMC has traded within a roughly 10-48x
forward P/E range, averaging around 20x.
The subsequent de-rating has been equally significant. EMC’s share price has fallen 56%
from its August peak, versus TAIEX down 21%, amid broader tech-sector selling, rising
concerns over slower AI spending, and uncertainty over whether PTFE adoption in

NVIDIA’s next-generation platforms could challenge EMC's high-end CCL positioning.
At NT$2,810 as of 20 November 2026, EMC trades at 10x/7x our 2027E/2028E EPS,
below its long-term historical median of 13.5x and well below its c.20x post-2023
average. Importantly, our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are already c.4%/4% below
consensus, partly reflecting a more conservative view amid rising AI demand concerns.
We believe the current valuation largely reflects these concerns, but gives insufficient

credit to EMC’s sustained technology leadership and earnings growth from continued
high-end material migration. Management expects M7+ to reach 70-80% of sales in
2027, versus 65-70% in Q326, while M9 is expected to enter mass production in 2027
and M10 could begin initial shipments in H127.
Our NT$5,000 price target is based on 12x 2028E P/E, at the mid to low end of EMC’s
7-20x historical valuation range. This compares with 8x for TUC versus its 7-17x

historical range and 10x for ITEQ versus its 9-19x historical range. We believe EMC’s
premium is warranted by its high-end material leadership, broader GPU and hyperscaler
ASIC exposure, and technology and capacity advantages. Our target multiple is also
above the 8x average for other CCL suppliers and 7x for PCB suppliers, while closer to
the 10x average for substrate suppliers as EMC expands its substrate CCL business.
EMC is trading at NT$2,810 (as of 20

Nov 2026)

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): We assume 2028E sales growth of 49.3%, driven by 62% CCL

growth, reflecting faster M9/M10 adoption, stronger AI server/switch demand and
sustained pricing. We assume a gross margin of 42.5% and EPS of NT$450.2. We apply

BASE (NT$5,000): We assume 2028E sales growth of 45.0%, driven by 57% CCL
growth, supported by continued AI demand, high-end material upgrades and capacity
expansion. We assume a gross margin of 42.0% and EPS of NT$430.7. We apply 12x

2028E P/E to derive our NT$5,000 base-case valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,650): We assume 2028E sales growth of 17.2%, driven by 22% CCL
growth, reflecting slower AI server/switch demand, delayed high-end material adoption
and an earlier-than-expected easing in CCL supply tightness. We assume a gross margin
of 36.8% and EPS of NT$253.8. We apply 7x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,650
downside valuation.

### Page 14

Elite Material UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional
FR-4 copper-clad thin-core laminate and prepreg manufacturer.
Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the world's largest
supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its
leading technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure
applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellites,
accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices

(20-25%), and automation, industrial and other applications (5-
10%).
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-
performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material
content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate

price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional FR-4 copper-clad thin-core
laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the
world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its leading
technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and

LEO satellites, accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and
automation, industrial and other applications (5-10%).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value EMC using a PE-based methodology.
Key risks include: 1) slower-than-expected high-end CCL adoption and AI infrastructure
deployments or delays in server upgrade cycles; 2) delays in capacity expansion or production
ramp-up; 3) stronger-than-expected competition and market-share losses in high-end CCL;

and 4) higher raw-material costs and weaker-than-expected cost pass-through to customers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G는 +11.5%, ASP는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

Document 254: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% YoY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% YoY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% YoY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다. 
목표주가 (유지) 2,000,000원

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Company Brief 
이차전지/석유화학 
Analyst 노우호 
wooho.rho@meritz.co.kr 
RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh 규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 510,000원 
현재주가 (10.14) 422,000원

Document 308: CEE & CCA week ahead_ Polish activity data and Czech confidence in focus

Source PDF: all report pdf/CEE & CCA week ahead_ Polish activity data and Czech confidence in focus.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Poland will release industrial output, labour market and retail
sales data, while Czech conﬁdence indicators are expected to
show weaker consumer sentiment but a modest improvement
in business conﬁdence

Adam Antoniak and David Havrlant
Poland: Industrial weakness persists as
consumption loses momentum
October industrial output and labour market data will be released
on Monday. Fewer working days than in October 2025 mean that
we should expect low-single-digit annual growth in industrial
output, as the underlying trend remains lacklustre and uneven. Cost
pressures in industry are mounting, pushing producer prices (PPI)
higher. Recent readings suggest that price pressures not only stem
from rising reﬁning prices but are becoming increasingly broad-
based. Nevertheless, this has not translated into stronger pressure
on core consumer prices, as weak demand is forcing businesses to
compress margins.

### Page 2

The labour market has stabilised, with wage growth in the
enterprise sector slowing to below 6% year-on-year and
employment continuing to decline. With demand for labour
somewhat weaker, supply-side deterioration, reﬂecting the
shrinking working-age population, is playing an increasingly
dominant role. The unemployment rate remains low and among
the lowest in the European Union.
October retail sales data follow on Tuesday. Fewer trading days and
a high reference base for food sales will outweigh some recovery in

fuel sales following another cut in VAT and excise duty on gasoline.
We also see limited room for stronger sales of durable goods, which
have proved surprisingly resilient to the strain on household
ﬁnances from higher fuel bills and slowing wage growth.
Consumption is projected to weaken further in the coming quarters
as elevated uncertainty keeps the propensity to save high.
Czech Republic: Consumer conﬁdence likely
weakened on higher food prices

Consumer conﬁdence likely deteriorated in November, with
Tuesday's release expected to reﬂect the impact of rising food
prices. That said, the consumer mood likely remained solidly above
its long-term average, as households continue to beneﬁt from
decent nominal and real wage dynamics. In contrast, business
conﬁdence likely edged up in the same month, somewhat
correcting its previous pronounced losses while remaining below its
long-term average, pressured by rising input costs and ongoing

uncertainty.

STRONG RESILIENCE TO EXTERNAL SHOCKS
, 
SOLID 
MACROECONOMIC FUNDAMENTALS 
◼ 
 Over the past 
 two years
 , India has demonstrated remarkable resilience in the face of external shocks, while its 
macroeconomic fundamentals have remained solid.  
◼ 
 In FY2025/2026  
(ended in March 2026)
 , India was hit by a sharp rise in US tariffs. However, thanks to supportive fiscal 
and monetary policies, 
 real GDP  
grew by 7.8% in FY2026, placing India among the fastest
 -
 growing Asian economies.  
◼ 
 For the current fiscal year, the Indian economy is facing rising energy prices 
 (
due to 
 the conflict in the Middle East and 
increased food prices 
 because 
 of 
an 
unfavourable monsoon
 ) 
as well as 
 monetary tightening. Nevertheless, despite these 
new challenges, real GDP growth is expected to exceed 7%.  
◼ 
 India is currently in a stronger position than it was in 2022 to cope with the new energy shock. The fiscal leeway to support  
the economy has increased with the decline in the fiscal deficit, and inflation is more contained.  
◼ 
 Although 
 rising  
energy prices have put pressure on external accounts, FX reserves 
 remain  
sufficient to cover the country’s 
short
 -
 term external financing needs. 
 Yet, 
downward pressures on the rupee have been 
 significant 
 and 
could persist in the 
short term
 . 
In 
H1 
2026, India’s external accounts withstood the rise in energy prices fairly well
 , notably  
thanks to the sharp increase 
in workers’ remittances. 
 T
he 
widening of the trade deficit was primarily due to the substantial increase in machinery and 
equipment imports 
 while t
 he oil trade deficit increased 
 only 
moderately, as the rise in energy prices was offset by a decline 
in import volumes. 
 However, 
in H2 2026, the oil trade deficit is expected to widen significantly.  
Additionally, large portfolio outflows have exacerbated downward pressure on the rupee, necessitating interventions by 
the central bank in foreign exchange markets and the adoption of regulatory measures to support the currency. Although 
these measures hav
 e led to a rebound in the rupee (at least temporarily), inflationary pressures, volatile portfolio 
investments, and low net foreign direct investments are 
 expected to continue 
 to put pressure on the currency.  
◼ 
 D
ownward pressures on the rupee 
 and 
 rising bond yields will reduce the government's 
 already limited 
 fiscal capacity to 
increase public investment and 
 support economic development. However, this will not undermine its ability to refinance 
its debt, given its safe structure.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector is sufficiently robust to cope with the expected rise in credit risks.

STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH DESPITE 
STRUCTURAL CONSTRAINTS 
◼ 
 In October 2026, the IMF revised upward 
 India’s  
medium
 -
 term growth 
 forecast 
 from 6.5% to 6.8%  
per year
 . Nonetheless, 
to achieve such strong growth performance, 
 India will have to adapt to the challenges posed by artificial intelligence  
(AI)
, 
while its infrastructure and education system are not prepared for it.  
◼ 
 Moreover, India’s development remains highly uneven. 
 The middle class is still modest
 . This is explained by a large 
informal economy, heavy concentration of employment in low value
 -
 added sectors such as agriculture, low participation 
rates among women and youth, and limited industrial development. All this, in turn, reflects India’s weak i
 ntegration into 
global value chains.  
◼ 
 India faces two key structural challenges: i
 ) adapting the services sector to AI and ii) accelerating industrial development. 
Even with progress in sectors such as smartphones and pharmaceuticals, India’s industrial base remains too small to 
absorb its vast labour force. 
 Labo
u
r market 
 rigidities  
and the mismatch between training and business needs are holding 
back both the creation of quality jobs and industrial development.  
◼ 
 Low net foreign direct investment is a 
 major  
 obstacle. While 
 India attracts 
 new 
 FDI, retention is weak
 : 
 despite 
improvements in logistics, governance issues and regulatory barriers have 
 led 
 disinvestments to triple over the past five 
years.  
◼ 
 India 
 may fail in completing its 
 transition to a higher
 -
 value, more industrialised and inclusive economy if reforms do not 
accelerate 
 significantly
 . Structural change
 s 
could lag behind pressures from its rapidly growing population and 
 fast 
global 
technological advances
 . 
02 November 2026

### Page 3

EcoInsight

economic
 -
 research.bnpparibas.com

3

INDIA
’S COMPLEX POSITION 
IN INTERNATIONAL TRADE 
Low 
attractiveness  
due to unfavourable business environment and limited integration in global value chains  
◼ 
 Despite very strong growth, India's attractiveness is low relative to the size of its economy. The stock of foreign direct 
investment stood at only 15% of GDP in Q2 2026 compared to 52% of GDP in Vietnam and around 67% of GDP in Thailand 
and Malaysia.  
◼ 
 This lack of appeal can be attributed to 
 India’s still unfavo
 u
rable 
 business environment and 
 its 
 limited integration into 
global value chains. India’s global market share remain
 s 
 low (1.9% of total 
 world 
 exports  
 of goods
 )
, 
 especially in 
comparison with that of China (16.1% of total exports).  
◼ 
 India has not benefited from the AI boom
 , 
since its exports of AI
 -
 related goods remain modest (0.5% of its total exports)
 . 
However, 
 it has benefited from the “China +1” strategy adopted by 
 international 
 companies seeking to diversify their 
production sources. Smartphone exports by India have increased 6.3
 -
 fold over the past five years and accounted for almost 
6% of global exports in 2025 compared to less than 1% five year
 s 
earlier.  
To 
increase its a
 ttractiveness and develop its industrial sector, India has 
 multiplied F
 ree 
T
rade 
 A
greements  
◼ 
 Over the past 18 months, 
 India  
has signed a growing number of free trade agreements 
 – 
with the European Free Trade 
Association in October 2025, the United Kingdom in July 2025, Oman in December 2025, and the European Union in January 
2026
. This 
 should help to develop the industrial base and demonstrates the Modi government's commitment to 
accelerating India's integration into global trade.  
The sectors most likely to benefit from the recent trade agreement with the EU are electronics and semiconductors, 
pharmaceuticals and chemical
 s
, textile and renewable energy technologies. But expanding trade agreements without deep 
domestic improvements in the business environment, governance, education, and infrastructure will be ineffective. India 
will continue to struggle with investment reten
 tion. 
◼ 
 The US
 -
 India bilateral trade agreement has not yet been concluded. The two sides reached a framework for an interim 
Agreement in February 2026, under which the US would apply an 18% reciprocal tariff on Indian goods, with further 
reductions subject to the 
 successful conclusion of the agreement. As of early October, negotiations 
 are 
stalled. Key sticking 
points include market access, particularly 
 in 
India’s sensitive agricultural sector.

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE

Non transfer risk   
India’s external accounts are solid with a low external debt, moderate current account deficit and large FX 
reserves. 
 India’s net debtor position reached only 5.9% of GDP in Q2 2026. In the short term, external accounts 
are expected to deteriorate 
 given 
the rise in energy prices while downward pressures on the rupee should remain 
high.  
◼ 
 External accounts have remained solid 
 despite recent adverse shocks
 . 
 In 2025, external accounts remained solid 
despite the significant increase in US tariffs. In 
 H1 
 2026, they held up well against rising energy prices. Although the 
current account deficit is expected to widen for the current fiscal year as a whole, it should remain contained, and foreign 
exchange reserves should be more than sufficient to cover the co
 untry’s short
 -
 term financing needs. The 
 major  
causes 
for concern are the 
 structurally 
 low level of foreign direct investment (although it is rising) and the increased reliance on 
portfolio investment, which exposes the currency to greater volatility.  
◼ 
 Structural vulnerability to energy price shock
 . 
India is structurally vulnerable to an energy price shock as its 
 oil and gas

Systemic credit risk  
The banking sector has strengthened over the past five years
 , 
as illustrated by the improvement in 
 asset  
quality 
and capital buffers. The main sources of credit risks are rising household debt, the rise in energy prices, the 
unfavourable monsoon and monetary tightening.  
◼ 
 The quality of assets has improved significantly. 
 In Q1 2026, the banking sector’s non
 -
 performing loan (NPL) ratio fell 
to a multi
 -
 decade low of 1.8%. For public banks  
(
54.4% of total loans
 )
, the NPL ratio declined to 2.1%.  
◼ 
 Capital buffers are strong. 
 While 
loan loss provisions remained modest at 75.6% of NPLs, capital buffers stayed strong. 
The Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR) rose to 17.7% in Q1 2026, up from 17.4% a year earlier. From April 2027, Indian banks 
will adopt a new Expected Credit Loss (ECL) framewo
 rk, requiring earlier provisioning for potential loan losses. In the 
short term, higher provisioning requirements could push up lending rates and tighten credit conditions 
 while 
 triggering 
capital raising or targeted government recapitalisation. 
 I
n the medium term, the reform could strengthen banking  
sector 
resilience through earlier recognition of credit risks and more robust loss buffers.

Document 336: First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-24).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10

### Page 1

#retail #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New
Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:19 November 23 2026 Share
The September quarter saw a larger than expected rise in spending, including firmness in
core categories.
September quarter retail sales
Retail sales (volume of goods sold): +2.2% (Prev: -0.4%)

### Page 2

Westpac f/c: 1.4%, Market: +0.5% 
Core retail sales (volume of goods sold): 1.4% (Prev: 0.8%)

Nominal retail sales: 2.4% (Prev: 1.0%)

Year to September
Volume of goods sold: 3.5%
Nominal sales: 7.0%

New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall.
The September quarter retail sales report showed a sharp recovery in spending. Nominal spending
was up 2.4% over the quarter, and the amount of goods that New Zealanders are actually taking home
was up a solid 2.2%.
Today’s result was above our top of market forecast for a 1.4% quarterly rise, and well above the
median market forecast for +0.5%.
While the strong September quarter result is welcome news, we are taking it with a grain of salt. Much
of the rise in spending was related to fuel spending, which was boosted by the fall in fuel prices over
the September quarter, and fuel prices have step higher since then.
However, even excluding fuel, we have seen firmness in spending appetites. Core spending (excl. fuel
and motor vehicles) was up 1.4% over the September quarter and is 4.7% higher than this time last
year. 
Looking at the breakdown of spending, we did see a rise in grocery purchases. Meanwhile, spending in
discretionary categories was mixed – there has been lower spending on items like furnishings and
hardware, while spending on hospitality, electronics and recreational items is up. Overall, were left with
a picture of gradually improving spending appetites.

Outlook
Today’s report reinforces our expectations that the economy is moving into stronger phase. We expect
that spending levels will continue to firm through the holiday season and 2027. However, that recovery
is still likely to be gradual due to the combination of ongoing high inflation, a soft labour market and
rising interest rates. We also expect the recovery will be uneven, with those regions with strong links to
the agricultural and tourism sectors outperforming metro regions, like Auckland and Wellington.

Implications for GDP growth
We’re forecasting GDP growth of 0.6% in the September quarter. Today’s result was above
expectations. We’ll take a closer look at how our forecast for GDP growth is shaping up over the next
couple of weeks as additional data on September quarter activity is released.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

化，将为 SpaceX 的 AI 收⼊来源提供多元化⽀撑。
尽管产品落地验证积极，Grok Bot 距离对 SpaceX 整体财务产⽣实质影响仍相距甚远。
据 SpaceX 招股说明书披露，其与 Anthropic 签订的云计算合同每⽉⾦额⾼达 12.5 亿美元。若以每
席位每⽉ 100 美元计算， SpaceX 需售出 1250 万个席位，才能与这⼀单⼀客⼾的⽉付款额持平。
分析师预计 SpaceX 营收将从今年的 490 亿美元增⻓⾄ 2027 年的 1820 亿美元、 2028 年的 3380 亿
美元。五个财务 Bot 的部署对上述预测不构成任何实质影响—— 即便是 2027 年预测值的 1% ，也
相当于 18.2 亿美元，约需 150 万个 100 美元席位全年持续付费⽅可覆盖。

从收⼊结构来看， SpaceX 的 AI 业务⾼度依赖单⼀⼤客⼾：据其季报披露，⼀名客⼾在 2026 年第
三季度贡献了 AI 业务收⼊的 34.5% ，且 Anthropic 合同双⽅均可在 90 天通知后终⽌。Grok Bot 席
位收⼊的战略价值，在于分散这⼀集中度⻛险，⽽⾮短期内替代⼤客⼾收⼊。
SpaceX 运营利润率已从 2024 年的 5.3% 下滑⾄ 2025 年的 -11.1% ， AI 业务板块当年运营亏损 64 亿
美元；随着新云计算合同上线，该板块在 2026 年第三季度实现 4.31 亿美元运营利润，逐步改
善。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

竞争对⼿同步跟进
SpaceX 进军 AI 财务⼯具赛道，正⾯遭遇两家成熟玩家的竞争。
Intuit⽅⾯， CEO Sasan Goodarzi 于 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇表⽰，公司 "AI 驱动的专家平台正在为消费者、企
业和会计师承担越来越多的⼯作 " 。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 31 ⽇的季度， QuickBooks Online 会计业务收⼊同
⽐增⻓ 19% ，略低于上⼀季度的 20% 。 Intuit 同⽇将全年营收增⻓预期从 9% ⾄ 10% 下调⾄ 8% ⾄
9% ，并将调整归因于 Credit Karma 业务。
Workday⽅⾯，其新推出的会计智能体专⻔针对成⻓型企业的⼈⼯审计准备⼯作。截⾄ 7 ⽉ 31 ⽇
的季度， AI 已驱动 Workday 逾 25% 的新年度合同价值，其 11 ⽉ 24 ⽇的财报将进⼀步揭⽰财务 Bot

是否正在压缩企业对其软件的⽀出。
综合来看，Grok Bot 已是⼀款经过内部验证的真实产品：它在 SpaceX ⾃⾝账⽬上运⾏，并已吸
引⾄少⼀个外部团队付费升级，这使其收⼊质量优于单⼀⼤客⼾模式。
但在 Anthropic 合同续约⻛险尚存、竞争对⼿加速布局的背景下， Grok Bot 的商业规模化仍需数
年时间。市场观察 Grok Bot 进展的最直接指标，将是 SpaceX 单⼀⼤客⼾收⼊占⽐能否从 34.5%
开始下降—— 这⼀数据将在 2027 年初的第四季度财报中得到初步验证。

Document 379: OPEC+将敲定2027年产量政策，伊朗战争与内部分歧加剧不确定性

Source PDF: all report pdf/OPEC+将敲定2027年产量政策，伊朗战争与内部分歧加剧不确定性.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

OPEC+ 将于本⽉底召开关键会议，讨论 2027 年产量政策。成员国产能独⽴评估结果将成为
新配额的制定基准，但伊拉克争取提⾼产量上限、阿联酋退出联盟，加之伊朗战争⾛向及
全球供应过剩⻛险，令产量分配⾯临复杂博弈。
摘要：
布伦特原油本⽉已下跌 25% ， OPEC+ 成员国正在就 2027 年产量政策展开年内最具决定性的⼀轮
谈判。
据彭博援引知情⼈⼠透露，本⽉底的部⻓级会议将以⼀份由达拉斯咨询机构 DeGolyer and
MacNaughton 完成的独⽴产能审计报告为基础，为各成员国设定新的产量基准。这些基准数字
将直接决定各国未来的增产空间，并对全球原油供需格局产⽣深远影响。
然⽽，包括伊朗战争⾛势、霍尔⽊兹海峡通⾏恢复速度以及全球需求前景在内的多重不确定因
素，令这次决策的难度空前复杂。
市场分析⼈⼠指出， 2027 年全球原油市场可能⾯临过剩压⼒。国际能源署等机构预测，届时全
球⽇产量或将超过消费量。与此同时，美国、圭亚那等⾮ OPEC 产油国仍在持续扩产，进⼀步压
缩了 OPEC+ 的政策腾挪空间。

油价⼤跌，但短期供应冲击或有限
布伦特原油期货本⽉累计跌幅接近 25% ，主要原因在于沙特及其他波斯湾出⼝国成功将原油以
战前价格转移出该地区，实体市场供应显得相对充裕。
尽管如此，油价较⼀年前仍⾼出约 40% ，这意味着⽬前并不存在迫切的价格压⼒驱使 OPEC+ ⼤
幅调整配额。分析⼈⼠预计，即便部分国家在此次审计中获得较⾼的新产能上限， OPEC+ 也不
太可能急于向市场释放额外供应。
Rystad Energy 地缘政治分析主管、前 OPEC 秘书处官员 Jorge Leon 表⽰：
"OPEC+ ⾯临极为艰难的抉择。该集团必须在美伊战争⾛势⾼度不明朗、霍尔⽊兹海峡恢复速度
难以预判，以及被战争打断的原油供应重返市场后潜在过剩规模等多重变量之间寻求平衡。 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

产能审计结果关乎基准起点，细节仍不透明
此次独⽴产能审计由 DeGolyer and MacNaughton Corp. 完成，旨在对联盟内部⻓期争议的核⼼
问题 —— 各成员国最⼤可持续产能 —— 给出独⽴评估数字。这⼀数字越⾼，该国主张增产的依
据就越充分。
11 ⽉ 16 ⽇， OPEC 秘书处官员与多国代表举⾏在线会议，宣布审计结束，各国获得了各⾃的评
估结果。
据代表团⼈⼠透露，这些数据将作为 2027 年 1 ⽉起新产量⽬标的 " 基准线 " ，但具体配额如何计算
仍不明确。
⼀名代表透露， 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇的会议时间较短，成员国⼏乎没有机会就新产能数字展开讨论。
他表⽰， OPEC+ 未向全体成员分发⼀份列明所有国家新产能数据的清单，令各国政府在进⼊会
议时⽆从得知本国产能在联盟中所处的相对位置，这⼀安排令其感到意外。
伊拉克施压争取更⾼基准，联盟向⼼⼒受考验
在此次审计中，争取更⾼产量基准最为积极的成员国是伊拉克。
今年 6 ⽉，伊拉克⽯油部曾短暂发出警告，称若产能评估结果不够理想，将考虑 " 是否留在或退

出 " 这个六⼗余年前在巴格达创⽴的组织。尽管该威胁随后被收回，但巴格达多年来⼀直对
OPEC 配额限制深感不满，并寻求重建其因战争重创的经济。
据知情⼈⼠ 9 ⽉透露，伊拉克寻求将产量基准定在每⽇ 600 万桶。
伊拉克的强硬⽴场令⼈格外警觉，原因在于此前数周，阿联酋刚刚宣布退出 OPEC+ ，理由是对
新油⽥项⽬产能受限感到失望。
据国际能源署数据，阿布扎⽐退出后产量已突破每⽇ 500 万桶，创下历史新⾼。历史上， 2023 年
对安哥拉的产能评估也曾引发争议，直接导致安哥拉在加⼊ OPEC16 年后宣布退出。
战争⾛势或⽐配额争议更具决定性影响
与产量⽬标的技术性博弈相⽐，伊朗战争的实际⾛向可能才是决定 OPEC 国家 2027 年实际产量
的最关键变量。
近期，尽管伊朗对霍尔⽊兹海峡过往船只的袭击有所升级，原油流通总体维持稳定。与此同
时，美国对伊朗船运维持严格封锁，对德⿊兰形成持续压⼒。
在此背景下，战前会议前，利雅得与巴格达均表⽰，其产量已从战时损失中恢复，并超过了各
⾃在 OPEC+ 框架下的配额上限。

RBC Capital Markets 分析师 Helima Croft 指出：
" 考虑到伊朗明确宣称要 ' 不惜⼀切⼿段打破封锁 ' ，该地区实弹交⽕的实际影响，在近期可能仍⽐
产量⽬标之争更具决定性意义—— 即便霍尔⽊兹海峡通⾏量近期有所回升，这⼀判断依然成
⽴。 "

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary: We retain our near-term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid-term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3-7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15-year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US? Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities among all scenarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer-term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fallible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bonds (CoCos1) 
and US 10-year government bond ideas: We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10-year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:  Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share-price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monetisation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence. We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight, while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya
